\section*{stat\_\allowbreak{}longterm\_\allowbreak{}measurement\_\allowbreak{}design}

\noindent\textit{Specialization:} v1\par

\paragraph{TL;DR.} Long-term data fusion requires choosing which short-term outcomes to measure, assigning their sequential proxy roles, and allocating a fixed budget between randomized short-term units and observational long-term follow-up.  This note makes that measurement design the argument of the two-source bridge score profile.  For the source-faithful Carneiro--Lee--Wilhelm affine-linear specialization, an explicit regular two-design family shows that the exact integer-budget population rule selects \(d_{\mathrm p}\) for every \(B\ge5\chi\), while both the arm-normalized and IKM-normalized bridge profiles select \(d_{\mathrm e}\).  The corresponding integer-selector ratios are unbounded on relatively open common-identification regions; at fixed \(\chi\) their center ratio has leading coefficient \((5\chi+1)/\{363(10\chi-3)\}\), and optimization over jointly feasible \((\chi,B)\) gives the sharp \(1/726\) elbow and cube-root cost scale.  An exact finite-catalogue pilot OLS minimizer corresponding to Carneiro--Lee--Wilhelm equation~(3.6) is root-\(m\) risk consistent yet selects the same inefficient design along the binary triangular path.  The positive prescription is to enumerate the observable bridge design value from a full-catalogue pilot, use the profiled square-root source allocation, and conduct selected-design-only Wald inference under the declared learner and variance conditions.

\section{Project justification}

\paragraph{Gap.} The long-term bridge and surrogate-efficiency literatures treat the measured proxy system and source ratio as fixed.  Carneiro--Lee--Wilhelm optimize affine predictive residual variance and sample size in a randomized-trial collection problem, but neither that work nor the fixed-system bridge literature supplies an efficiency guarantee for transporting this objective to costly sequential proxy designs, an exact integer-budget comparison, or an analysis of its finite-catalogue empirical least-squares minimizer under persistent confounding.

\paragraph{Niche.} A finite costed catalogue with preattached sequential roles creates nonnested inverse problems with separate experimental and observational information bottlenecks.  Exact floor counts and a feasible pilot selector make it possible to distinguish failure of the affine predictive objective from identification failure or population-oracle infeasibility.

\paragraph{Fill.} The note gives a source-faithful nonredundant full-Gram transport of the Carneiro--Lee--Wilhelm affine-linear criterion, proves its continuous and exact integer-budget rules, and constructs a regular common-identification region on which every \(B\ge5\chi\) selects the wrong bridge design with unbounded arm-normalized and IKM ratios.  It carries the exact center formula to the integer ratios, separates the fixed-\(\chi\) coefficient from the jointly feasible optimized \(1/726\) elbow, and proves that the exact finite-catalogue pilot OLS minimizer corresponding to equation~(3.6) is root-\(m\) consistent but inherits the divergent ratio.  Generic unrestricted-risk selectors remain secondary comparisons; bridge-value enumeration, source allocation, and conditional post-selection Wald inference provide the positive design rule.

\section{Related work}

ImbensKallusMaoWang2025 supplies the persistent-confounding bridge model, the fixed-system source-score limit law, and the local efficiency theorem under its identification, uniqueness, bijectivity, and fixed-ratio premises.  This note treats the measured subset, sequential role assignment, and source allocation as design arguments selected by an independent pilot, and invokes absolute causal efficiency only through the separately cited fixed-system result.  CarneiroLeeWilhelm2020, Section~3.2, Assumption~3.1, Lemma~3.1, and equations~(3.3)--(3.6), supplies the balanced-treatment affine-linear residual-variance-over-sample-size criterion and its pre-experimental empirical least-squares counterpart.  The present transport maps support only to \(M_d\), keeps bridge roles preattached, uses linear paired-unit costs and exact floor counts, and analyzes the exact finite-catalogue OLS minimizer corresponding to equation~(3.6); it does not evaluate the original randomized-trial theorem or approximate OGA and does not attribute persistent-confounding analysis or bridge-role choice to Carneiro--Lee--Wilhelm.  KallusMao2025 compares efficiency with and without a fixed surrogate vector rather than optimizing a costly subset-role system.  TaoZengLin2020 and WangWangMiao2023 optimize two-phase sampling for fixed measured variables or a fixed full-data influence-function setting, whereas this note selects proxy subset, sequential role assignment, and source allocation.  GuptaLiptonChilders2024 learns allocations for a supplied moment system.  BennettKallusMaoNeweySyrgkanisUehara2023 studies inference on strongly identified functionals of weakly identified nuisances, and ZhangLiMiaoTchetgenTchetgen2023 develops proximal inference without bridge uniqueness; this note instead maintains unique bridges, bijective operators, and explicit weak--strong learner rates and claims neither weak-identification nor nonuniqueness robustness.  ChernozhukovEtAl2018 supplies cross-fitting and orthogonal product-rate machinery, while the present inference contribution is independent-pilot design and allocation selection, selected-design-only fitting, and conditional random-index Wald inference.  YangEcklesDhillonAral2024 and LalImbensHull2026 are contextual comparisons about predictive and causal relevance, and MareisDrton2026 treats cost-aware staged measurement in a linear front-door model rather than the two-bridge long-term fusion problem.

\section{Setup}

Target (estimand / structural parameter): The causal estimand is \(\tau=\mathbb E_P[Y(1)-Y(0)\mid G=O]\). The observable statistical target selected by the main theorem is \(\theta_{\widehat d_m}(P)=\mu_{\widehat d_m,1}-\mu_{\widehat d_m,0}\) on \(\mathfrak P_{\mathcal D}^{\mathrm{obs}}\); it equals \(\tau\) only on \(\mathfrak P_{\mathcal D}^{\mathrm{id}}\), which contains the binary witness region but not all of \(\mathfrak P_{\mathcal D}\)..
Primary functional / estimator / diagnostic: The main statistical functional is the selected observed bridge contrast \(\theta_{\widehat d_m}(P)\) on \(\mathfrak P_{\mathcal D}^{\mathrm{obs}}\). Its explicit selected-design cross-fitted estimator, studentizer, limit law, and Wald interval are given by Definition~\(\mathrm{def{:}selected\text{-}wald\text{-}variance}\) and Theorem~\(\mathrm{thm{:}postselection\text{-}wald}\), conditional on the declared selected-path learner, multiplier, pilot, and variance conditions. Proposition~\(\mathrm{prop{:}postselection\text{-}wald\text{-}ate\text{-}corollary}\) converts this to inference for \(\tau\) on \(\mathfrak P_{\mathcal D}^{\mathrm{id}}\). Absolute fixed-system causal efficiency uses the IKM-normalized moments and the separately cited canonical-gradient conditions..
\begin{itemize}
  \item \texttt{K} --- \texttt{catalogue\_\allowbreak{}size}; \(\mathbb N\); \texttt{index}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The finite number of candidate short-\allowbreak{}term outcomes.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal J} --- \texttt{index\_\allowbreak{}set}; \(\{1,\ldots,K\}\); \texttt{index}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The catalogue index set \(\mathcal J=\{1,\ldots,K\}\).
  \item \texttt{a} --- \texttt{treatment\_\allowbreak{}level}; \(\{0,1\}\); \texttt{index}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{A generic treatment level.}
  \item \texttt{g} --- \texttt{sample\_\allowbreak{}label}; \(\{E,O\}\); \texttt{index}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{A generic experimental or observational sample label.}
  \item \texttt{G} --- \texttt{sample\_\allowbreak{}indicator}; \(\{E,O\}\); \texttt{datum}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The indicator of membership in the experimental sample \(E\) or observational long-term-follow-up sample \(O\).
  \item \texttt{X} --- \texttt{baseline\_\allowbreak{}covariate}; \(\mathcal X\); \texttt{datum}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{Observed pretreatment covariates.}
  \item \texttt{U} --- \texttt{latent\_\allowbreak{}confounder}; \(\mathcal U_0\); \texttt{latent}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The persistent unobserved confounder in the observational population.}
  \item \texttt{A} --- \texttt{treatment}; \(\{0,1\}\); \texttt{datum}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The realized binary treatment.}
  \item \texttt{T} --- \texttt{signed\_\allowbreak{}treatment}; \(\{-1,1\}\); \texttt{derived\_\allowbreak{}datum}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The signed treatment \(T=2A-1\).
  \item \texttt{s} --- \texttt{binary\_\allowbreak{}proxy\_\allowbreak{}signal}; \((0,1)\); \texttt{index}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} A generic signal strength for a binary proxy of \(U\).
  \item \texttt{W\_\allowbreak{}s} --- \texttt{binary\_\allowbreak{}proxy\_\allowbreak{}family}; \(\{-1,1\}\); \texttt{witness\_\allowbreak{}proxy}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} A binary proxy whose conditional mass is \(\Pr(W_s=w\mid U=u)=(1+suw)/2\) for \(u,w\in\{-1,1\}\).
  \item \texttt{Y(a)} --- \texttt{potential\_\allowbreak{}long\_\allowbreak{}term\_\allowbreak{}outcome}; \(L_2\); \texttt{potential\_\allowbreak{}outcome}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The long-term potential outcome under treatment \(a\).
  \item \texttt{Y} --- \texttt{realized\_\allowbreak{}long\_\allowbreak{}term\_\allowbreak{}outcome}; \(\mathbb R\); \texttt{datum}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The realized long-term outcome, observed only when \(G=O\).
  \item \texttt{Z(a)} --- \texttt{potential\_\allowbreak{}short\_\allowbreak{}term\_\allowbreak{}catalogue}; \(\prod_{j\in\mathcal J}\mathcal Z_j\); \texttt{potential\_\allowbreak{}outcome}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The full vector \(Z(a)=(Z_j(a):j\in\mathcal J)\) of candidate short-term potential outcomes under treatment \(a\), ordered by measurement time.
  \item \texttt{Z} --- \texttt{realized\_\allowbreak{}short\_\allowbreak{}term\_\allowbreak{}catalogue}; \(\prod_{j\in\mathcal J}\mathcal Z_j\); \texttt{datum}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The realized full-catalogue vector \(Z=Z(A)\).
  \item \texttt{P} --- \texttt{full\_\allowbreak{}catalogue\_\allowbreak{}law}; \(\mathcal P(G,X,U,A,Y(0),Y(1),Z(0),Z(1))\); \texttt{law}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The full-\allowbreak{}catalogue superpopulation law.}
  \item \texttt{c\_\allowbreak{}j} --- \texttt{measurement\_\allowbreak{}cost}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{cost}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The known per-unit cost of measuring catalogue outcome \(Z_j\).
  \item \texttt{b\_\allowbreak{}E} --- \texttt{base\_\allowbreak{}experimental\_\allowbreak{}cost}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{cost}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The known base cost of one experimental unit before catalogue measurements.}
  \item \texttt{b\_\allowbreak{}O} --- \texttt{base\_\allowbreak{}followup\_\allowbreak{}cost}; \((b_E,\infty)\); \texttt{cost}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The known base cost of one observational long-term-follow-up unit, including measurement of \(Y\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}kappa} --- \texttt{per\_\allowbreak{}unit\_\allowbreak{}measurement\_\allowbreak{}cap}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{budget}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The cap on the catalogue-\allowbreak{}measurement cost incurred for any main-\allowbreak{}study unit.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal D} --- \texttt{admissible\_\allowbreak{}design\_\allowbreak{}family}; \texttt{finite nonempty set}; \texttt{decision\_\allowbreak{}set}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The ex-ante-known finite family of pairs \(d=(M_d,r_d)\), where \(M_d\subseteq\mathcal J\), \(\sum_{j\in M_d}c_j\le\kappa\), and \(r_d:M_d\to\{1,2,3\}\) assigns every measured outcome to one sequential role.
  \item \texttt{d} --- \texttt{measurement\_\allowbreak{}design}; \(\mathcal D\); \texttt{decision}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{A generic admissible measured-\allowbreak{}subset and role-\allowbreak{}assignment pair.}
  \item \texttt{d'} --- \texttt{measurement\_\allowbreak{}design}; \(\mathcal D\); \texttt{decision}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{A second generic admissible measurement design used in pairwise comparisons.}
  \item \texttt{M\_\allowbreak{}d} --- \texttt{measured\_\allowbreak{}subset}; \(2^{\mathcal J}\); \texttt{decision\_\allowbreak{}component}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The catalogue subset measured under design \(d\).
  \item \texttt{r\_\allowbreak{}d} --- \texttt{role\_\allowbreak{}assignment}; \(M_d\to\{1,2,3\}\); \texttt{decision\_\allowbreak{}component}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The sequential-role assignment under design \(d\).
  \item \texttt{S\_\allowbreak{}\{1d\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{early\_\allowbreak{}short\_\allowbreak{}term\_\allowbreak{}potential\_\allowbreak{}and\_\allowbreak{}realized\_\allowbreak{}vector}; \texttt{product space}; \texttt{proxy}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The role-one potential subvector \(S_{1d}(a)=(Z_j(a):j\in M_d,\ r_d(j)=1)\) and its realized version \(S_{1d}=S_{1d}(A)\).
  \item \texttt{S\_\allowbreak{}\{2d\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{middle\_\allowbreak{}short\_\allowbreak{}term\_\allowbreak{}potential\_\allowbreak{}and\_\allowbreak{}realized\_\allowbreak{}vector}; \texttt{product space}; \texttt{sequential\_\allowbreak{}separator}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The role-two potential subvector \(S_{2d}(a)=(Z_j(a):j\in M_d,\ r_d(j)=2)\) and its realized version \(S_{2d}=S_{2d}(A)\).
  \item \texttt{S\_\allowbreak{}\{3d\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{late\_\allowbreak{}short\_\allowbreak{}term\_\allowbreak{}potential\_\allowbreak{}and\_\allowbreak{}realized\_\allowbreak{}vector}; \texttt{product space}; \texttt{proxy}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The role-three potential subvector \(S_{3d}(a)=(Z_j(a):j\in M_d,\ r_d(j)=3)\) and its realized version \(S_{3d}=S_{3d}(A)\).
  \item \texttt{p\_\allowbreak{}\{g,\allowbreak{}a\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{treatment\_\allowbreak{}probability}; \((0,1)\); \texttt{nuisance}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The sample-specific probability \(p_{g,a}=P(A=a\mid G=g)\).
  \item \texttt{h\_\allowbreak{}d} --- \texttt{outcome\_\allowbreak{}bridge}; \(L_2(S_{3d},S_{2d},A,X)\); \texttt{nuisance}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The unique square-integrable solution \(h_d\) of \(\mathbb E_P[Y\mid S_{2d},S_{1d},A,X,G=O]=\mathbb E_P[h_d(S_{3d},S_{2d},A,X)\mid S_{2d},S_{1d},A,X,G=O]\).
  \item \texttt{q\_\allowbreak{}d} --- \texttt{selection\_\allowbreak{}bridge}; \(L_2(S_{2d},S_{1d},A,X)\); \texttt{nuisance}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{A design-\allowbreak{}specific square-\allowbreak{}integrable candidate selection bridge.}
  \item \texttt{T\_\allowbreak{}d} --- \texttt{conditional\_\allowbreak{}expectation\_\allowbreak{}operator}; \(L_2(S_{3d},S_{2d},A,X)\to L_2(S_{2d},S_{1d},A,X)\); \texttt{operator}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The operator \((T_df)(S_{2d},S_{1d},A,X)=\mathbb E_P[f(S_{3d},S_{2d},A,X)\mid S_{2d},S_{1d},A,X,G=O]\).
  \item \texttt{T\_\allowbreak{}d\textasciicircum{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}star} --- \texttt{adjoint\_\allowbreak{}conditional\_\allowbreak{}expectation\_\allowbreak{}operator}; \(L_2(S_{2d},S_{1d},A,X)\to L_2(S_{3d},S_{2d},A,X)\); \texttt{operator}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The adjoint operator \((T_d^\star u)(S_{3d},S_{2d},A,X)=\mathbb E_P[u(S_{2d},S_{1d},A,X)\mid S_{3d},S_{2d},A,X,G=O]\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}|\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}cdot\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}|\_\allowbreak{}\{2,\allowbreak{}O,\allowbreak{}h,\allowbreak{}d\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{strong\_\allowbreak{}outcome\_\allowbreak{}bridge\_\allowbreak{}norm}; \texttt{seminorm}; \texttt{error\_\allowbreak{}metric}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} For an outcome-bridge perturbation \(f\), \(\|f\|_{2,O,h,d}=\{\mathbb E_P[f(S_{3d},S_{2d},A,X)^2\mid G=O]\}^{1/2}\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}|\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}cdot\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}|\_\allowbreak{}\{2,\allowbreak{}O,\allowbreak{}q,\allowbreak{}d\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{strong\_\allowbreak{}selection\_\allowbreak{}bridge\_\allowbreak{}norm}; \texttt{seminorm}; \texttt{error\_\allowbreak{}metric}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} For a selection-bridge perturbation \(u\), \(\|u\|_{2,O,q,d}=\{\mathbb E_P[u(S_{2d},S_{1d},A,X)^2\mid G=O]\}^{1/2}\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}|\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}cdot\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}|\_\allowbreak{}\{w,\allowbreak{}h,\allowbreak{}d\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{weak\_\allowbreak{}outcome\_\allowbreak{}bridge\_\allowbreak{}seminorm}; \texttt{seminorm}; \texttt{error\_\allowbreak{}metric}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The weak outcome-bridge seminorm \(\|f\|_{w,h,d}=\|T_df\|_{2,O,q,d}\), which measures violation of the design-specific observed outcome-bridge equation.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}|\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}cdot\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}|\_\allowbreak{}\{w,\allowbreak{}q,\allowbreak{}d\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{weak\_\allowbreak{}selection\_\allowbreak{}bridge\_\allowbreak{}seminorm}; \texttt{seminorm}; \texttt{error\_\allowbreak{}metric}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The weak selection-bridge seminorm \(\|u\|_{w,q,d}=\|T_d^\star u\|_{2,O,h,d}\), which measures violation of the design-specific observed selection-bridge equation.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mu\_\allowbreak{}\{d,\allowbreak{}a\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{bridge\_\allowbreak{}mean}; \(\mathbb R\); \texttt{nuisance}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The experimental bridge mean \(\mu_{d,a}=\mathbb E_P[h_d(S_{3d},S_{2d},a,X)\mid A=a,G=E]\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}tau} --- \texttt{causal\_\allowbreak{}estimand}; \(\mathbb R\); \texttt{estimand}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The observational-population long-term average treatment effect \(\tau=\mathbb E_P[Y(1)-Y(0)\mid G=O]\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}phi\_\allowbreak{}\{E,\allowbreak{}d\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{experimental\_\allowbreak{}gradient\_\allowbreak{}component}; \(L_2^0(P(\cdot\mid G=E))\); \texttt{influence\_\allowbreak{}component}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The experimental score \(\phi_{E,d}=\sum_{a=0}^1(-1)^{1-a}\mathbf 1\{A=a\}p_{E,a}^{-1}\{h_d(S_{3d},S_{2d},a,X)-\mu_{d,a}\}\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}phi\_\allowbreak{}\{O,\allowbreak{}d\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{observational\_\allowbreak{}gradient\_\allowbreak{}component}; \(L_2^0(P(\cdot\mid G=O))\); \texttt{influence\_\allowbreak{}component}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The observational score \(\phi_{O,d}=\sum_{a=0}^1(-1)^{1-a}\mathbf 1\{A=a\}p_{O,a}^{-1}q_d(S_{2d},S_{1d},a,X)\{Y-h_d(S_{3d},S_{2d},a,X)\}\).
  \item \texttt{V\_\allowbreak{}\{E,\allowbreak{}d\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{experimental\_\allowbreak{}information\_\allowbreak{}component}; \([0,\infty]\); \texttt{efficiency\_\allowbreak{}component}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The variance component \(V_{E,d}=\mathbb E_P[\phi_{E,d}^2\mid G=E]\).
  \item \texttt{V\_\allowbreak{}\{O,\allowbreak{}d\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{observational\_\allowbreak{}information\_\allowbreak{}component}; \([0,\infty]\); \texttt{efficiency\_\allowbreak{}component}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The variance component \(V_{O,d}=\mathbb E_P[\phi_{O,d}^2\mid G=O]\).
  \item \texttt{c\_\allowbreak{}\{E,\allowbreak{}d\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{experimental\_\allowbreak{}unit\_\allowbreak{}cost}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{cost}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The per-unit cost \(c_{E,d}=b_E+\sum_{j\in M_d}c_j\).
  \item \texttt{c\_\allowbreak{}\{O,\allowbreak{}d\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{followup\_\allowbreak{}unit\_\allowbreak{}cost}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{cost}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The per-unit cost \(c_{O,d}=b_O+\sum_{j\in M_d}c_j\).
  \item \texttt{B} --- \texttt{main\_\allowbreak{}study\_\allowbreak{}budget}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{asymptotic\_\allowbreak{}index}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The total monetary budget of the main study.}
  \item \texttt{n\_\allowbreak{}E} --- \texttt{fixed\_\allowbreak{}experimental\_\allowbreak{}sample\_\allowbreak{}size}; \(\mathbb N\); \texttt{sample\_\allowbreak{}size}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{A generic fixed experimental sample size in the anchor reduction.}
  \item \texttt{n\_\allowbreak{}O} --- \texttt{fixed\_\allowbreak{}observational\_\allowbreak{}sample\_\allowbreak{}size}; \(\mathbb N\); \texttt{sample\_\allowbreak{}size}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{A generic fixed observational sample size in the anchor reduction.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}alpha} --- \texttt{experimental\_\allowbreak{}budget\_\allowbreak{}share}; \((0,1)\); \texttt{allocation}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The fraction of \(B\) spent on experimental units.
  \item \texttt{n\_\allowbreak{}\{E,\allowbreak{}B\}\allowbreak{}(d,\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}alpha)} --- \texttt{experimental\_\allowbreak{}sample\_\allowbreak{}size}; \(\mathbb N\); \texttt{sample\_\allowbreak{}size}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The feasible count \(n_{E,B}(d,\alpha)=\lfloor\alpha B/c_{E,d}\rfloor\).
  \item \texttt{n\_\allowbreak{}\{O,\allowbreak{}B\}\allowbreak{}(d,\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}alpha)} --- \texttt{observational\_\allowbreak{}sample\_\allowbreak{}size}; \(\mathbb N\); \texttt{sample\_\allowbreak{}size}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The feasible count \(n_{O,B}(d,\alpha)=\lfloor(1-\alpha)B/c_{O,d}\rfloor\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathfrak r\_\allowbreak{}\{d,\allowbreak{}B\}\allowbreak{}(\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}alpha;\allowbreak{}P)} --- \texttt{integer\_\allowbreak{}rounding\_\allowbreak{}residual}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{rounding\_\allowbreak{}error}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The floor-count residual \(\mathfrak r_{d,B}(\alpha;P)=B\{V_{E,d}/n_{E,B}(d,\alpha)+V_{O,d}/n_{O,B}(d,\alpha)\}-\{c_{E,d}V_{E,d}/\alpha+c_{O,d}V_{O,d}/(1-\alpha)\}\) whenever both integer counts are positive.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal V\_\allowbreak{}d(\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}alpha;\allowbreak{}P)} --- \texttt{budget\_\allowbreak{}normalized\_\allowbreak{}efficiency\_\allowbreak{}bound}; \((0,\infty]\); \texttt{focal\_\allowbreak{}object}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The design-value profile \(\mathcal V_d(\alpha;P)=c_{E,d}V_{E,d}/\alpha+c_{O,d}V_{O,d}/(1-\alpha)\) for regular \(d\), and \(+\infty\) otherwise.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}alpha\_\allowbreak{}d\textasciicircum{}*(P)} --- \texttt{profiled\_\allowbreak{}budget\_\allowbreak{}share}; \((0,1)\); \texttt{optimal\_\allowbreak{}allocation}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The optimizer \(\alpha_d^*(P)=\sqrt{c_{E,d}V_{E,d}}/(\sqrt{c_{E,d}V_{E,d}}+\sqrt{c_{O,d}V_{O,d}})\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal V\_\allowbreak{}d\textasciicircum{}*(P)} --- \texttt{profiled\_\allowbreak{}design\_\allowbreak{}value}; \((0,\infty]\); \texttt{focal\_\allowbreak{}object}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The profiled value \(\mathcal V_d^*(P)=\inf_{0<\alpha<1}\mathcal V_d(\alpha;P)\).
  \item \texttt{D\textasciicircum{}*(P)} --- \texttt{oracle\_\allowbreak{}measurement\_\allowbreak{}design}; \(2^{\mathcal D}\); \texttt{optimal\_\allowbreak{}design}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The oracle set \(D^*(P)=\arg\min_{d\in\mathcal D}\mathcal V_d^*(P)\), retaining every minimizer under ties.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}gamma(P)} --- \texttt{oracle\_\allowbreak{}separation}; \((0,\infty]\); \texttt{selection\_\allowbreak{}margin}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The scalar separation \(\gamma(P)=\min_{d\notin D^*(P)}\{\mathcal V_d^*(P)-\min_{d'\in\mathcal D}\mathcal V_{d'}^*(P)\}\), with \(\gamma(P)=+\infty\) when \(D^*(P)=\mathcal D\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}Delta\_\allowbreak{}P(d,\allowbreak{}d')} --- \texttt{design\_\allowbreak{}value\_\allowbreak{}gap}; \(\mathbb R\); \texttt{selection\_\allowbreak{}criterion}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The pairwise gap \(\Delta_P(d,d')=\mathcal V_d^*(P)-\mathcal V_{d'}^*(P)\).
  \item \texttt{R\_\allowbreak{}d(P)} --- \texttt{predictive\_\allowbreak{}risk}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{benchmark\_\allowbreak{}objective}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The best observational long-term prediction risk \(R_d(P)=\inf_{f\in L_2(X,Z_{M_d})}\mathbb E_P[(Y-f(X,Z_{M_d}))^2\mid G=O]\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal D\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{pred\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}(P)} --- \texttt{raw\_\allowbreak{}prediction\_\allowbreak{}risk\_\allowbreak{}optimizer\_\allowbreak{}set}; \(2^{\mathcal D}\); \texttt{benchmark\_\allowbreak{}decision}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The minimizer set \(\mathcal D_{\mathrm{pred}}(P)=\arg\min_{d\in\mathcal D}R_d(P)\), retaining every tie.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}rho\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{pred\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}(P)} --- \texttt{raw\_\allowbreak{}prediction\_\allowbreak{}risk\_\allowbreak{}approximation\_\allowbreak{}ratio}; \([1,\infty]\); \texttt{diagnostic}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The best-\allowbreak{}tie profiled design-\allowbreak{}value ratio of the raw population prediction-\allowbreak{}risk selector.}
  \item \texttt{m} --- \texttt{pilot\_\allowbreak{}sample\_\allowbreak{}size}; \(\mathbb N\); \texttt{asymptotic\_\allowbreak{}index}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The full-\allowbreak{}catalogue pilot sample size.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}eta} --- \texttt{test\_\allowbreak{}level}; \((0,1)\); \texttt{confidence\_\allowbreak{}level}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The nominal noncoverage probability for the post-\allowbreak{}selection Wald interval.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal P\_\allowbreak{}m} --- \texttt{full\_\allowbreak{}catalogue\_\allowbreak{}pilot}; \texttt{sample}; \texttt{pilot\_\allowbreak{}data}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} An independent pilot of size \(m\) observing \(G,X,A,Z,Y\mathbf 1\{G=O\}\), with every component of \(Z\) measured.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat V\_\allowbreak{}\{E,\allowbreak{}d\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{pilot\_\allowbreak{}experimental\_\allowbreak{}variance\_\allowbreak{}estimator}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{estimator}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The cross-fitted pilot second-moment estimator of \(V_{E,d}\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat V\_\allowbreak{}\{O,\allowbreak{}d\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{pilot\_\allowbreak{}observational\_\allowbreak{}variance\_\allowbreak{}estimator}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{estimator}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The cross-fitted pilot second-moment estimator of \(V_{O,d}\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal V\}\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}\{d,\allowbreak{}m\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}*} --- \texttt{pilot\_\allowbreak{}design\_\allowbreak{}value\_\allowbreak{}estimator}; \([0,\infty]\); \texttt{estimator}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The cross-fitted plug-in version of \(\mathcal V_d^*(P)\) obtained by fitting \(h_d,q_d\) and the two score variances on \(\mathcal P_m\), then inserting the known costs.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat d\_\allowbreak{}m} --- \texttt{plugin\_\allowbreak{}measurement\_\allowbreak{}design}; \(\mathcal D\); \texttt{estimator}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} A deterministic-tie-broken selector \(\widehat d_m\in\arg\min_{d\in\mathcal D}\widehat{\mathcal V}_{d,m}^*\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}underline v\_\allowbreak{}m} --- \texttt{variance\_\allowbreak{}truncation}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{regularization}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} A deterministic sequence satisfying \(\underline v_m\downarrow0\), used only to keep the pilot allocation in the interior in finite samples.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}alpha\_\allowbreak{}\{d,\allowbreak{}m\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{pilot\_\allowbreak{}allocation\_\allowbreak{}rule}; \((0,1)\); \texttt{estimator}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The pilot-feasible share \(\widehat\alpha_{d,m}=\sqrt{c_{E,d}(\widehat V_{E,d}\vee\underline v_m)}/[\sqrt{c_{E,d}(\widehat V_{E,d}\vee\underline v_m)}+\sqrt{c_{O,d}(\widehat V_{O,d}\vee\underline v_m)}]\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}alpha\_\allowbreak{}m} --- \texttt{selected\_\allowbreak{}pilot\_\allowbreak{}allocation}; \((0,1)\); \texttt{estimator}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The implemented main-study share \(\widehat\alpha_m=\widehat\alpha_{\widehat d_m,m}\).
  \item \texttt{N\_\allowbreak{}\{E,\allowbreak{}B\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{implemented\_\allowbreak{}experimental\_\allowbreak{}count}; \(\mathbb N\); \texttt{sample\_\allowbreak{}size}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The pilot-feasible integer count \(N_{E,B}=\lfloor\widehat\alpha_m B/c_{E,\widehat d_m}\rfloor\).
  \item \texttt{N\_\allowbreak{}\{O,\allowbreak{}B\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{implemented\_\allowbreak{}observational\_\allowbreak{}count}; \(\mathbb N\); \texttt{sample\_\allowbreak{}size}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The pilot-feasible integer count \(N_{O,B}=\lfloor(1-\widehat\alpha_m)B/c_{O,\widehat d_m}\rfloor\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}varepsilon\_\allowbreak{}m} --- \texttt{uniform\_\allowbreak{}pilot\_\allowbreak{}error}; \([0,\infty]\); \texttt{error}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The error \(\varepsilon_m=\max_{d\in\mathcal D}|\widehat{\mathcal V}_{d,m}^*-\mathcal V_d^*(P)|\).
  \item \texttt{L} --- \texttt{crossfit\_\allowbreak{}fold\_\allowbreak{}count}; \(\mathbb N\); \texttt{index}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{A fixed number of main-\allowbreak{}study cross-\allowbreak{}fitting folds.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat h\_\allowbreak{}\{d,\allowbreak{}B\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}\{(-\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}ell)\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{crossfit\_\allowbreak{}outcome\_\allowbreak{}bridge\_\allowbreak{}estimator}; \(L_2(S_{3d},S_{2d},A,X)\); \texttt{nuisance\_\allowbreak{}estimator}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The outcome-bridge estimator for design \(d\) trained off main-study fold \(\ell\in\{1,\ldots,L\}\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat q\_\allowbreak{}\{d,\allowbreak{}B\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}\{(-\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}ell)\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{crossfit\_\allowbreak{}selection\_\allowbreak{}bridge\_\allowbreak{}estimator}; \(L_2(S_{2d},S_{1d},A,X)\); \texttt{nuisance\_\allowbreak{}estimator}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The selection-bridge estimator for design \(d\) trained off main-study fold \(\ell\in\{1,\ldots,L\}\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}delta\_\allowbreak{}\{h,\allowbreak{}B\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{outcome\_\allowbreak{}bridge\_\allowbreak{}weak\_\allowbreak{}rate}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{rate}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{A deterministic uniform weak-\allowbreak{}seminorm rate for the main-\allowbreak{}study outcome bridges.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}rho\_\allowbreak{}\{h,\allowbreak{}B\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{outcome\_\allowbreak{}bridge\_\allowbreak{}strong\_\allowbreak{}rate}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{rate}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{A deterministic uniform strong-\allowbreak{}norm rate for the main-\allowbreak{}study outcome bridges.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}delta\_\allowbreak{}\{q,\allowbreak{}B\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{selection\_\allowbreak{}bridge\_\allowbreak{}weak\_\allowbreak{}rate}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{rate}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{A deterministic uniform weak-\allowbreak{}seminorm rate for the main-\allowbreak{}study selection bridges.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}rho\_\allowbreak{}\{q,\allowbreak{}B\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{selection\_\allowbreak{}bridge\_\allowbreak{}strong\_\allowbreak{}rate}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{rate}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{A deterministic uniform strong-\allowbreak{}norm rate for the main-\allowbreak{}study selection bridges.}
  \item \texttt{r\_\allowbreak{}\{V,\allowbreak{}m\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{pilot\_\allowbreak{}variance\_\allowbreak{}rate}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{rate}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{A deterministic rate for uniform pilot estimation of the two source-\allowbreak{}specific gradient variances.}
  \item \texttt{r\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widetilde V,\allowbreak{}B\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{main\_\allowbreak{}study\_\allowbreak{}variance\_\allowbreak{}rate}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{rate}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{A deterministic rate for uniform main-\allowbreak{}study estimation of the two source-\allowbreak{}specific gradient variances.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}epsilon} --- \texttt{weak\_\allowbreak{}proxy\_\allowbreak{}signal}; \((0,10^{-3})\); \texttt{perturbation\_\allowbreak{}index}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The signal strength of the early proxy in the prediction-\allowbreak{}selected witness design.}
  \item \texttt{d\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm p\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{prediction\_\allowbreak{}selected\_\allowbreak{}design}; \(\mathcal D\); \texttt{witness\_\allowbreak{}design}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The witness design using the weak early proxy and the strong late proxy.}
  \item \texttt{d\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm e\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{efficiency\_\allowbreak{}selected\_\allowbreak{}design}; \(\mathcal D\); \texttt{witness\_\allowbreak{}design}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The witness design using two moderately informative proxies.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathsf H\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{comp\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{raw\_\allowbreak{}prediction\_\allowbreak{}risk\_\allowbreak{}comparison\_\allowbreak{}handle}; \texttt{method}; \texttt{open\_\allowbreak{}question\_\allowbreak{}handle}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The quadratic-\allowbreak{}form comparison handle for the generic raw population prediction-\allowbreak{}risk rule.}
  \item \texttt{z\_\allowbreak{}\{1-\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}eta/\allowbreak{}2\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{standard\_\allowbreak{}normal\_\allowbreak{}quantile}; \(\mathbb R\); \texttt{critical\_\allowbreak{}value}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The \(1-\eta/2\) quantile of the standard normal distribution.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}psi\_\allowbreak{}\{E,\allowbreak{}d\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{IKM\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{ikmw\_\allowbreak{}experimental\_\allowbreak{}gradient\_\allowbreak{}component}; \(L_2^0(P(\cdot\mid G=E))\); \texttt{influence\_\allowbreak{}component}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The Imbens--Kallus--Mao--Wang-normalized experimental score \(\psi_{E,d}^{\mathrm{IKM}}=(1-p_{E,1})\phi_{E,d}\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}psi\_\allowbreak{}\{O,\allowbreak{}d\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{IKM\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{ikmw\_\allowbreak{}observational\_\allowbreak{}gradient\_\allowbreak{}component}; \(L_2^0(P(\cdot\mid G=O))\); \texttt{influence\_\allowbreak{}component}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The Imbens--Kallus--Mao--Wang-normalized observational score \(\psi_{O,d}^{\mathrm{IKM}}=(1-p_{O,1})\phi_{O,d}\).
  \item \texttt{V\_\allowbreak{}\{E,\allowbreak{}d\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{IKM\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{ikmw\_\allowbreak{}experimental\_\allowbreak{}information\_\allowbreak{}component}; \([0,\infty]\); \texttt{efficiency\_\allowbreak{}component}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The IKM-normalized experimental source moment \(V_{E,d}^{\mathrm{IKM}}=\mathbb E_P[(\psi_{E,d}^{\mathrm{IKM}})^2\mid G=E]=(1-p_{E,1})^2V_{E,d}\).
  \item \texttt{V\_\allowbreak{}\{O,\allowbreak{}d\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{IKM\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{ikmw\_\allowbreak{}observational\_\allowbreak{}information\_\allowbreak{}component}; \([0,\infty]\); \texttt{efficiency\_\allowbreak{}component}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The IKM-normalized observational source moment \(V_{O,d}^{\mathrm{IKM}}=\mathbb E_P[(\psi_{O,d}^{\mathrm{IKM}})^2\mid G=O]=(1-p_{O,1})^2V_{O,d}\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal V\_\allowbreak{}\{d,\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{IKM\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}*(P)} --- \texttt{ikmw\_\allowbreak{}profiled\_\allowbreak{}design\_\allowbreak{}value}; \((0,\infty]\); \texttt{focal\_\allowbreak{}object}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The profiled IKM-normalized value \(\mathcal V_{d,\mathrm{IKM}}^*(P)=(\sqrt{c_{E,d}V_{E,d}^{\mathrm{IKM}}}+\sqrt{c_{O,d}V_{O,d}^{\mathrm{IKM}}})^2\).
  \item \texttt{D\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{IKM\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}*(P)} --- \texttt{ikmw\_\allowbreak{}oracle\_\allowbreak{}measurement\_\allowbreak{}design}; \(2^{\mathcal D}\); \texttt{optimal\_\allowbreak{}design}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The IKM-normalized oracle set \(D_{\mathrm{IKM}}^*(P)=\arg\min_{d\in\mathcal D}\mathcal V_{d,\mathrm{IKM}}^*(P)\), retaining all ties.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}rho\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{pred\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{IKM\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}(P)} --- \texttt{ikmw\_\allowbreak{}raw\_\allowbreak{}prediction\_\allowbreak{}risk\_\allowbreak{}ratio}; \([1,\infty]\); \texttt{diagnostic}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The best-\allowbreak{}tie ratio of the raw population prediction-\allowbreak{}risk selector computed with the IKM-\allowbreak{}normalized profile.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}theta\_\allowbreak{}d(P)} --- \texttt{observed\_\allowbreak{}bridge\_\allowbreak{}contrast}; \(\mathbb R\); \texttt{estimand\_\allowbreak{}functional}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The identified designwise observed-data bridge contrast \(\theta_d(P)=\mu_{d,1}-\mu_{d,0}\). It equals the causal target only under an additional common-identification condition.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}sigma\_\allowbreak{}\{d,\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{IKM\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}2} --- \texttt{ikmw\_\allowbreak{}local\_\allowbreak{}efficiency\_\allowbreak{}bound}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{efficiency\_\allowbreak{}bound}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The Imbens--Kallus--Mao--Wang fixed-system local efficiency lower bound for design \(d\) and causal target \(\tau\).
  \item \texttt{n} --- \texttt{combined\_\allowbreak{}sample\_\allowbreak{}size}; \(\mathbb N\); \texttt{sample\_\allowbreak{}size}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The combined fixed-system sample size \(n=n_E+n_O\).
  \item \texttt{P\_\allowbreak{}n\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}circ\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{artificial\_\allowbreak{}two\_\allowbreak{}source\_\allowbreak{}law}; \texttt{probability law}; \texttt{comparison\_\allowbreak{}law}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The artificial law with source masses \(n_E/n\) and \(n_O/n\) and source-conditional distributions inherited from \(P\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}lambda} --- \texttt{source\_\allowbreak{}sample\_\allowbreak{}ratio}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{asymptotic\_\allowbreak{}ratio}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The limiting fixed-system ratio \(n_E/n_O\).
  \item \texttt{L\_\allowbreak{}0} --- \texttt{fixed\_\allowbreak{}design\_\allowbreak{}fold\_\allowbreak{}count}; \(\{2,3,\ldots\}\); \texttt{index}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The fixed number of observational-\allowbreak{}only cross-\allowbreak{}fitting folds in the published fixed-\allowbreak{}design comparison.}
  \item \texttt{S\_\allowbreak{}1} --- \texttt{fixed\_\allowbreak{}system\_\allowbreak{}early\_\allowbreak{}outcome}; \texttt{product space}; \texttt{proxy}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The early short-\allowbreak{}term outcome in the fixed-\allowbreak{}system IKM comparison.}
  \item \texttt{S\_\allowbreak{}2} --- \texttt{fixed\_\allowbreak{}system\_\allowbreak{}middle\_\allowbreak{}outcome}; \texttt{product space}; \texttt{sequential\_\allowbreak{}separator}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The middle short-\allowbreak{}term outcome in the fixed-\allowbreak{}system IKM comparison.}
  \item \texttt{S\_\allowbreak{}3} --- \texttt{fixed\_\allowbreak{}system\_\allowbreak{}late\_\allowbreak{}outcome}; \texttt{product space}; \texttt{proxy}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The late short-\allowbreak{}term outcome in the fixed-\allowbreak{}system IKM comparison.}
  \item \texttt{h\_\allowbreak{}0} --- \texttt{fixed\_\allowbreak{}system\_\allowbreak{}outcome\_\allowbreak{}bridge}; \(L_2(S_3,S_2,A,X)\); \texttt{nuisance}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The true outcome bridge in the cited fixed-\allowbreak{}system theorem.}
  \item \texttt{q\_\allowbreak{}0} --- \texttt{fixed\_\allowbreak{}system\_\allowbreak{}selection\_\allowbreak{}bridge}; \(L_2(S_2,S_1,A,X)\); \texttt{nuisance}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The true selection bridge in the cited fixed-\allowbreak{}system theorem.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mu\_\allowbreak{}0} --- \texttt{fixed\_\allowbreak{}system\_\allowbreak{}bridge\_\allowbreak{}mean}; map from \(\{0,1\}\) to \(\mathbb R\); \texttt{nuisance}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The treatment-\allowbreak{}indexed bridge mean in the cited fixed-\allowbreak{}system score.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathsf T\_\allowbreak{}0} --- \texttt{fixed\_\allowbreak{}system\_\allowbreak{}conditional\_\allowbreak{}expectation\_\allowbreak{}operator}; \(L_2(S_3,S_2,A,X)\to L_2(S_2,S_1,A,X)\); \texttt{operator}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The conditional-\allowbreak{}expectation operator in the cited fixed-\allowbreak{}system theorem.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathsf T\_\allowbreak{}0\textasciicircum{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}star} --- \texttt{fixed\_\allowbreak{}system\_\allowbreak{}adjoint\_\allowbreak{}operator}; \(L_2(S_2,S_1,A,X)\to L_2(S_3,S_2,A,X)\); \texttt{operator}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The adjoint of \(\mathsf T_0\) in the cited fixed-system theorem.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat h\textasciicircum{}\{(-\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}ell)\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{fixed\_\allowbreak{}system\_\allowbreak{}crossfit\_\allowbreak{}outcome\_\allowbreak{}bridge}; \(L_2(S_3,S_2,A,X)\); \texttt{nuisance\_\allowbreak{}estimator}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The outcome-bridge fit trained outside fixed-system fold \(\ell\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat q\textasciicircum{}\{(-\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}ell)\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{fixed\_\allowbreak{}system\_\allowbreak{}crossfit\_\allowbreak{}selection\_\allowbreak{}bridge}; \(L_2(S_2,S_1,A,X)\); \texttt{nuisance\_\allowbreak{}estimator}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The selection-bridge fit trained outside fixed-system fold \(\ell\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}delta\_\allowbreak{}\{h,\allowbreak{}n\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{fixed\_\allowbreak{}system\_\allowbreak{}outcome\_\allowbreak{}weak\_\allowbreak{}rate}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{rate}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The weak outcome-\allowbreak{}bridge rate in the cited fixed-\allowbreak{}design result.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}rho\_\allowbreak{}\{h,\allowbreak{}n\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{fixed\_\allowbreak{}system\_\allowbreak{}outcome\_\allowbreak{}strong\_\allowbreak{}rate}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{rate}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The strong outcome-\allowbreak{}bridge rate in the cited fixed-\allowbreak{}design result.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}delta\_\allowbreak{}\{q,\allowbreak{}n\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{fixed\_\allowbreak{}system\_\allowbreak{}selection\_\allowbreak{}weak\_\allowbreak{}rate}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{rate}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The weak selection-\allowbreak{}bridge rate in the cited fixed-\allowbreak{}design result.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}rho\_\allowbreak{}\{q,\allowbreak{}n\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{fixed\_\allowbreak{}system\_\allowbreak{}selection\_\allowbreak{}strong\_\allowbreak{}rate}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{rate}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The strong selection-\allowbreak{}bridge rate in the cited fixed-\allowbreak{}design result.}
  \item \texttt{V\_\allowbreak{}E\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{IKM\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{fixed\_\allowbreak{}system\_\allowbreak{}ikmw\_\allowbreak{}experimental\_\allowbreak{}moment}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{efficiency\_\allowbreak{}component}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The experimental score second moment printed in IKM Theorem 5 for a fixed short-\allowbreak{}term system.}
  \item \texttt{V\_\allowbreak{}O\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{IKM\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{fixed\_\allowbreak{}system\_\allowbreak{}ikmw\_\allowbreak{}observational\_\allowbreak{}moment}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{efficiency\_\allowbreak{}component}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The observational score second moment printed in IKM Theorem 5 for a fixed short-\allowbreak{}term system.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}tau\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{DR\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{ikmw\_\allowbreak{}fixed\_\allowbreak{}system\_\allowbreak{}estimator}; \(\mathbb R\); \texttt{estimator}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The doubly robust estimator in Imbens-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}Kallus-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}Mao-\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}Wang Theorem 5.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal I\_\allowbreak{}\{E,\allowbreak{}B\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{experimental\_\allowbreak{}index\_\allowbreak{}set}; \texttt{finite index set}; \texttt{index\_\allowbreak{}set}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The main-study experimental indices, of cardinality \(N_{E,B}\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal I\_\allowbreak{}\{O,\allowbreak{}B\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{observational\_\allowbreak{}index\_\allowbreak{}set}; \texttt{finite index set}; \texttt{index\_\allowbreak{}set}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The main-study observational indices, of cardinality \(N_{O,B}\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal I\_\allowbreak{}\{O,\allowbreak{}B,\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}ell\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{observational\_\allowbreak{}fold\_\allowbreak{}index\_\allowbreak{}set}; \texttt{finite index set}; \texttt{index\_\allowbreak{}set}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} Observational evaluation fold \(\ell\) in a deterministic balanced partition of \(\mathcal I_{O,B}\).
  \item \texttt{e\_\allowbreak{}\{B,\allowbreak{}a\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{experimental\_\allowbreak{}arm\_\allowbreak{}count}; \(\mathbb N_0\); \texttt{sample\_\allowbreak{}size}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The number of experimental main-study observations assigned treatment \(a\).
  \item \texttt{o\_\allowbreak{}\{B,\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}ell,\allowbreak{}a\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{observational\_\allowbreak{}fold\_\allowbreak{}arm\_\allowbreak{}count}; \(\mathbb N_0\); \texttt{sample\_\allowbreak{}size}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The number of observations in observational fold \(\ell\) with treatment \(a\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat p\_\allowbreak{}\{E,\allowbreak{}a\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{empirical\_\allowbreak{}experimental\_\allowbreak{}arm\_\allowbreak{}probability}; \([0,1]\); \texttt{estimator}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The empirical experimental arm proportion \(e_{B,a}/N_{E,B}\), assigned zero if its source count vanishes.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat p\_\allowbreak{}\{O,\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}ell,\allowbreak{}a\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{empirical\_\allowbreak{}observational\_\allowbreak{}fold\_\allowbreak{}arm\_\allowbreak{}probability}; \([0,1]\); \texttt{estimator}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The empirical arm proportion within observational fold \(\ell\), assigned zero if its fold size vanishes.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mu\_\allowbreak{}\{d,\allowbreak{}a\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}\{(-\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}ell)\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{foldwise\_\allowbreak{}experimental\_\allowbreak{}bridge\_\allowbreak{}mean}; \(\mathbb R\); \texttt{estimator}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The experimental arm mean of the fold-\(\ell\) outcome-bridge fit, with zero-denominator value zero.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mu\_\allowbreak{}\{B,\allowbreak{}d\}\allowbreak{}(a)} --- \texttt{crossfit\_\allowbreak{}bridge\_\allowbreak{}mean\_\allowbreak{}estimator}; \(\mathbb R\); \texttt{estimator}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The average of experimental bridge predictions and observational bridge residual corrections for treatment \(a\), totalized at zero denominators.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}tau\_\allowbreak{}B(d,\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}alpha)} --- \texttt{design\_\allowbreak{}allocation\_\allowbreak{}bridge\_\allowbreak{}contrast\_\allowbreak{}estimator}; \(\mathbb R\); \texttt{estimator}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The cross-fitted contrast \(\widehat\mu_{B,d}(1)-\widehat\mu_{B,d}(0)\) at design \(d\) and allocation \(\alpha\), under the declared total convention.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}phi\_\allowbreak{}\{E,\allowbreak{}d,\allowbreak{}B,\allowbreak{}i\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{experimental\_\allowbreak{}plugin\_\allowbreak{}score}; \(\mathbb R\); \texttt{estimated\_\allowbreak{}influence\_\allowbreak{}component}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The exact arm-normalized fold-averaged experimental plug-in score for observation \(i\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}phi\_\allowbreak{}\{O,\allowbreak{}d,\allowbreak{}B,\allowbreak{}i\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{observational\_\allowbreak{}plugin\_\allowbreak{}score}; \(\mathbb R\); \texttt{estimated\_\allowbreak{}influence\_\allowbreak{}component}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The exact arm-normalized observational plug-in score for observation \(i\), evaluated with its fold-external fits.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widetilde V\_\allowbreak{}\{g,\allowbreak{}d,\allowbreak{}B\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{total\_\allowbreak{}main\_\allowbreak{}study\_\allowbreak{}score\_\allowbreak{}variance\_\allowbreak{}estimator}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{variance\_\allowbreak{}estimator}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The within-source empirical second moment of the exact arm-normalized plug-in score \(\widehat\phi_{g,d,B,i}\), with value zero when its source count or a required arm denominator is zero.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathsf D\_\allowbreak{}B} --- \texttt{positive\_\allowbreak{}denominator\_\allowbreak{}event}; \texttt{event}; \texttt{event}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The event that both selected source counts and every experimental arm and observational fold-\allowbreak{}arm count are positive.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat s\_\allowbreak{}B\textasciicircum{}2} --- \texttt{total\_\allowbreak{}post\_\allowbreak{}selection\_\allowbreak{}variance\_\allowbreak{}estimator}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{variance\_\allowbreak{}estimator}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The total variance estimator \(\mathbf 1\{\mathsf D_B\}B\{\widetilde V_{E,\widehat d_m,B}/N_{E,B}+\widetilde V_{O,\widehat d_m,B}/N_{O,B}\}\), with all zero-denominator fractions assigned zero.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat s\_\allowbreak{}B} --- \texttt{total\_\allowbreak{}post\_\allowbreak{}selection\_\allowbreak{}standard\_\allowbreak{}error}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{standard\_\allowbreak{}error}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The nonnegative square root \((\widehat s_B^2)^{1/2}\), hence zero outside \(\mathsf D_B\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathsf G\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{tot\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{total\_\allowbreak{}wald\_\allowbreak{}event}; \texttt{event}; \texttt{event}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The event \(\mathsf D_B\cap\{\widehat s_B^2>0\}\) on which the ordinary studentized statistic is evaluated.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}tau\_\allowbreak{}B} --- \texttt{total\_\allowbreak{}post\_\allowbreak{}selection\_\allowbreak{}bridge\_\allowbreak{}estimator}; \(\mathbb R\); \texttt{estimator}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} On \(\mathsf G_{\mathrm{tot}}\), the explicit cross-fitted estimator \(\widehat\tau_B(\widehat d_m,\widehat\alpha_m)\); on its complement, zero. It targets \(\theta_{\widehat d_m}(P)\) on the full regular class and \(\tau\) under common identification.
  \item \texttt{I\_\allowbreak{}B} --- \texttt{total\_\allowbreak{}postselection\_\allowbreak{}wald\_\allowbreak{}interval}; random closed interval in \(\mathbb R\); \texttt{confidence\_\allowbreak{}interval}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} On \(\mathsf G_{\mathrm{tot}}\), the interval \([\widehat\tau_B-z_{1-\eta/2}\widehat s_B/\sqrt B,\widehat\tau_B+z_{1-\eta/2}\widehat s_B/\sqrt B]\); on the complement, \([0,0]\).
  \item \texttt{s\_\allowbreak{}\{0,\allowbreak{}B\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{oracle\_\allowbreak{}selected\_\allowbreak{}standard\_\allowbreak{}error}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{oracle\_\allowbreak{}standard\_\allowbreak{}error}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The nonnegative square root of \(B\{V_{E,\widehat d_m}/N_{E,B}+V_{O,\widehat d_m}/N_{O,B}\}\) on positive source counts and zero otherwise.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathsf T\_\allowbreak{}B} --- \texttt{total\_\allowbreak{}studentized\_\allowbreak{}statistic}; \(\mathbb R\); \texttt{test\_\allowbreak{}statistic}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} On \(\mathsf G_{\mathrm{tot}}\), the statistic \(\sqrt B\{\widehat\tau_B-\theta_{\widehat d_m}(P)\}/\widehat s_B\), and zero otherwise.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal C\_\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}kappa} --- \texttt{induced\_\allowbreak{}catalogue\_\allowbreak{}cost\_\allowbreak{}manifold}; subset of \((0,\infty)^{K+2}\); \texttt{parameter\_\allowbreak{}region}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The cap-feasible primitive cost vectors \((b_E,b_O,c_1,\ldots,c_K)\) inducing every \(c_{E,d}\) and \(c_{O,d}\) through the catalogue subset formulas.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}tau\_\allowbreak{}0} --- \texttt{ikmw\_\allowbreak{}fixed\_\allowbreak{}system\_\allowbreak{}target}; \(\mathbb R\); \texttt{estimand}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The fixed-\allowbreak{}system causal target in the cited IKM limit theorem.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}|\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}cdot\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}|\_\allowbreak{}2} --- \texttt{fixed\_\allowbreak{}system\_\allowbreak{}l2\_\allowbreak{}norm}; \texttt{norm}; \texttt{error\_\allowbreak{}metric}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The \(L_2(P_n^{\circ})\) norm in the cited fixed-design comparison.
  \item \texttt{e\_\allowbreak{}B} --- \texttt{deterministic\_\allowbreak{}path\_\allowbreak{}experimental\_\allowbreak{}count}; \(\mathbb N_0\); \texttt{sample\_\allowbreak{}size}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The floor count \(\lfloor\alpha_BB/c_{E,d_B}\rfloor\) on a deterministic design-allocation path.
  \item \texttt{o\_\allowbreak{}B} --- \texttt{deterministic\_\allowbreak{}path\_\allowbreak{}observational\_\allowbreak{}count}; \(\mathbb N_0\); \texttt{sample\_\allowbreak{}size}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The floor count \(\lfloor(1-\alpha_B)B/c_{O,d_B}\rfloor\) on a deterministic design-allocation path.
  \item \texttt{n\_\allowbreak{}B} --- \texttt{deterministic\_\allowbreak{}path\_\allowbreak{}total\_\allowbreak{}count}; \(\mathbb N_0\); \texttt{sample\_\allowbreak{}size}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The total deterministic-path count \(n_B=e_B+o_B\).
  \item \texttt{P\_\allowbreak{}\{B,\allowbreak{}d,\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}alpha\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}circ\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{deterministic\_\allowbreak{}path\_\allowbreak{}two\_\allowbreak{}source\_\allowbreak{}law}; \texttt{probability law}; \texttt{comparison\_\allowbreak{}law}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The artificial law with source masses proportional to \(e_B,o_B\) and source-conditional laws inherited from \(P\) along design \(d\) and share \(\alpha\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}|\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}cdot\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}|\_\allowbreak{}\{2,\allowbreak{}B\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}circ\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{deterministic\_\allowbreak{}path\_\allowbreak{}l2\_\allowbreak{}norm}; \texttt{norm}; \texttt{error\_\allowbreak{}metric}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The \(L_2(P_{B,d,\alpha}^{\circ})\) norm used only for functions having declared versions on both sources or for terms explicitly zeroed outside \(G=O\); it is not applied to an observational equivalence class lacking a declared experimental-source version.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}chi} --- \texttt{prescribed\_\allowbreak{}cost\_\allowbreak{}disadvantage\_\allowbreak{}factor}; \((1,\infty)\); \texttt{index}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The arbitrary prescribed finite paired-\allowbreak{}cost ratio of the prediction-\allowbreak{}selected witness design to the efficient witness design.}
  \item \texttt{y\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}chi\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{late\_\allowbreak{}proxy\_\allowbreak{}signal}; \((0,1)\); \texttt{witness\_\allowbreak{}proxy\_\allowbreak{}signal}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The late-proxy signal \(y_{\chi}=\sqrt{1-3/(10\chi)}\) in the prediction-selected center design.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal M\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{bin\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}(\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}chi)} --- \texttt{binary\_\allowbreak{}constrained\_\allowbreak{}finite\_\allowbreak{}cell\_\allowbreak{}model}; \texttt{set of finite-\allowbreak{}support laws}; \texttt{witness\_\allowbreak{}model}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The \(\chi\)-indexed constrained finite-cell family defined in Definition \(\mathrm{def{:}binary\text{-}witness}\), with relative topology induced by the complete latent-cell vector.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathfrak P\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal D\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{obs\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{observable\_\allowbreak{}bridge\_\allowbreak{}law\_\allowbreak{}class}; \texttt{set of observed two-\allowbreak{}source laws}; \texttt{model\_\allowbreak{}class}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The observable bridge-law class in Definition \(\mathrm{def{:}observable\text{-}bridge\text{-}law\text{-}class}\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathfrak P\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal D\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{regular\_\allowbreak{}law\_\allowbreak{}class}; \texttt{set of laws}; \texttt{model\_\allowbreak{}class}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The stronger causal full-catalogue subclass of \(\mathfrak P_{\mathcal D}^{\mathrm{obs}}\), containing the latent exchangeability, external-validity, sequential-outcome, and completeness restrictions for every design.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal U\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{bin\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{regular\_\allowbreak{}witness\_\allowbreak{}region}; \(2^{\mathfrak P_{\mathcal D}}\); \texttt{witness\_\allowbreak{}region}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The relatively open finite-cell perturbation region around the binary witness path specified in Definition \(\mathrm{def{:}binary\text{-}region}\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal U\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{bin\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{IKM\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{ikmw\_\allowbreak{}regular\_\allowbreak{}witness\_\allowbreak{}region}; \(2^{\mathfrak P_{\mathcal D}}\); \texttt{witness\_\allowbreak{}region}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The relatively open subregion of \(\mathcal U_{\mathrm{bin}}\) on which the strict IKM-normalized optimizer ordering is preserved.
  \item \texttt{P\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}epsilon\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{finite\_\allowbreak{}support\_\allowbreak{}witness\_\allowbreak{}law}; \(\mathfrak P_{\mathcal D}\); \texttt{witness\_\allowbreak{}law}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The \(\chi\)-indexed binary finite-support center law specified in Definition \(\mathrm{def{:}binary\text{-}witness}\).
  \item \texttt{r\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}epsilon\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{binary\_\allowbreak{}stability\_\allowbreak{}radius}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{radius}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The jointly shrunken regularity, optimizer, and exact-integer residual-margin stability radius at \(P_\epsilon\).
  \item \texttt{r\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}epsilon\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{opt\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{binary\_\allowbreak{}optimizer\_\allowbreak{}stability\_\allowbreak{}radius}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{radius}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The alias \(r_{\epsilon}^{\mathrm{opt}}=r_{\epsilon}\) used by the corrected binary-region definition.
  \item \texttt{r\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}epsilon\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{IKM\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{binary\_\allowbreak{}ikmw\_\allowbreak{}stability\_\allowbreak{}radius}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{radius}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The jointly shrunken regularity, common-identification, strict IKM-optimizer, and exact-integer IKM-ratio stability radius at \(P_\epsilon\).
  \item \texttt{P\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}chi,\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}epsilon\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{finite\_\allowbreak{}support\_\allowbreak{}witness\_\allowbreak{}law}; \(\mathfrak P_{\mathcal D}\); \texttt{witness\_\allowbreak{}law}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The binary finite-support center law indexed jointly by the prescribed cost-disadvantage factor \(\chi\) and early-proxy signal \(\epsilon\); for fixed \(\chi\), it is the law denoted \(P_\epsilon\) in Definition \(\mathrm{def{:}binary\text{-}witness}\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathfrak P\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal D\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{id\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{common\_\allowbreak{}identification\_\allowbreak{}class}; subset of \(\mathfrak P_{\mathcal D}\); \texttt{model\_\allowbreak{}class}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The causal subclass on which \(\theta_d(P)=\tau\) for every admissible design.
  \item \texttt{R\_\allowbreak{}d\textasciicircum{}A(P)} --- \texttt{treatment\_\allowbreak{}aware\_\allowbreak{}predictive\_\allowbreak{}risk}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{benchmark\_\allowbreak{}objective}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The best observational long-\allowbreak{}term squared-\allowbreak{}error prediction risk using treatment,\allowbreak{} baseline covariates,\allowbreak{} and the measured catalogue subset.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal D\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{pred\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}A(P)} --- \texttt{treatment\_\allowbreak{}aware\_\allowbreak{}prediction\_\allowbreak{}optimizer\_\allowbreak{}set}; \(2^{\mathcal D}\); \texttt{benchmark\_\allowbreak{}decision}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The tie-preserving minimizer set of \(R_d^A(P)\) over the admissible family.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}rho\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{pred\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}A(P)} --- \texttt{treatment\_\allowbreak{}aware\_\allowbreak{}prediction\_\allowbreak{}source\_\allowbreak{}score\_\allowbreak{}ratio}; \([1,\infty]\); \texttt{diagnostic}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The best-\allowbreak{}tie arm-\allowbreak{}normalized profiled design-\allowbreak{}value ratio of the treatment-\allowbreak{}aware population prediction-\allowbreak{}risk selector.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}rho\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{pred\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}\{A,\allowbreak{}\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{IKM\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}(P)} --- \texttt{treatment\_\allowbreak{}aware\_\allowbreak{}prediction\_\allowbreak{}ikmw\_\allowbreak{}ratio}; \([1,\infty]\); \texttt{diagnostic}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{The best-\allowbreak{}tie IKM-\allowbreak{}normalized profiled design-\allowbreak{}value ratio of the treatment-\allowbreak{}aware population prediction-\allowbreak{}risk selector.}
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat f\_\allowbreak{}\{d,\allowbreak{}m\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}A} --- \texttt{saturated\_\allowbreak{}treatment\_\allowbreak{}aware\_\allowbreak{}pilot\_\allowbreak{}regression}; \texttt{finite-\allowbreak{}cell regression map}; \texttt{estimator}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The training-half saturated observational cell-mean predictor of \(Y\) from \(A,X,Z_{M_d}\) in the full-catalogue pilot.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat R\_\allowbreak{}\{d,\allowbreak{}m\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}A} --- \texttt{heldout\_\allowbreak{}treatment\_\allowbreak{}aware\_\allowbreak{}risk\_\allowbreak{}estimator}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{estimator}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The validation-half empirical squared loss of \(\widehat f_{d,m}^A\) on observational full-catalogue pilot records.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}widehat d\_\allowbreak{}m\textasciicircum{}A} --- \texttt{empirical\_\allowbreak{}treatment\_\allowbreak{}aware\_\allowbreak{}measurement\_\allowbreak{}design}; \(\mathcal D\); \texttt{estimator}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The deterministically tie-broken minimizer of \(\widehat R_{d,m}^A\) over the admissible family.
  \item \texttt{X\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{nr\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{nonredundant\_\allowbreak{}affine\_\allowbreak{}covariate\_\allowbreak{}encoding}; \texttt{finite-\allowbreak{}dimensional real vector space}; \texttt{benchmark\_\allowbreak{}regressor}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} A fixed nonredundant encoding of the always-measured covariates, with constant and reference columns removed. If \(X\) has one-point support, then \(X_{\mathrm{nr}}\) is zero-dimensional.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathfrak P\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal D\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{CLW\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}} --- \texttt{clw\_\allowbreak{}admissible\_\allowbreak{}transported\_\allowbreak{}domain}; subset of \(\mathfrak P_{\mathcal D}^{\mathrm{obs}}\); \texttt{benchmark\_\allowbreak{}domain}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The laws satisfying the finite-moment and full-Gram conditions of Definition \(\mathrm{def{:}clw\text{-}linear\text{-}cost\text{-}selector}\), under the source-faithful balanced-treatment, arm-homoskedastic, and linear per-unit cost specialization stated there.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}xi\_\allowbreak{}d} --- \texttt{clw\_\allowbreak{}affine\_\allowbreak{}regressor}; \texttt{finite-\allowbreak{}dimensional real vector}; \texttt{benchmark\_\allowbreak{}regressor}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The nonredundant affine regressor \(\xi_d=(1,X_{\mathrm{nr}}^\top,Z_{M_d}^\top)^\top\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}Gamma\_\allowbreak{}d(P)} --- \texttt{clw\_\allowbreak{}full\_\allowbreak{}gram\_\allowbreak{}matrix}; \texttt{positive definite finite matrix on the admissible domain}; \texttt{benchmark\_\allowbreak{}moment}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The full observational Gram matrix \(\Gamma_d(P)=\mathbb E_P[\xi_d\xi_d^\top\mid G=O]\).
  \item \texttt{g\_\allowbreak{}d(P)} --- \texttt{clw\_\allowbreak{}outcome\_\allowbreak{}cross\_\allowbreak{}moment}; \texttt{finite-\allowbreak{}dimensional real vector}; \texttt{benchmark\_\allowbreak{}moment}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The observational outcome cross moment \(g_d(P)=\mathbb E_P[\xi_dY\mid G=O]\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}beta\_\allowbreak{}d\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{lin\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}(P)} --- \texttt{clw\_\allowbreak{}affine\_\allowbreak{}projection\_\allowbreak{}coefficient}; \texttt{finite-\allowbreak{}dimensional real vector}; \texttt{benchmark\_\allowbreak{}nuisance}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The population affine least-squares coefficient \(\beta_d^{\mathrm{lin}}(P)=\Gamma_d(P)^{-1}g_d(P)\) on \(\mathfrak P_{\mathcal D}^{\mathrm{CLW}}\).
  \item \texttt{r\_\allowbreak{}d\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{lin\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}(P)} --- \texttt{clw\_\allowbreak{}affine\_\allowbreak{}linear\_\allowbreak{}residual\_\allowbreak{}variance}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{named\_\allowbreak{}benchmark\_\allowbreak{}risk}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The population affine residual variance \(r_d^{\mathrm{lin}}(P)=\mathbb E_P[(Y-\xi_d^\top\beta_d^{\mathrm{lin}}(P))^2\mid G=O]\).
  \item \texttt{C\_\allowbreak{}d} --- \texttt{clw\_\allowbreak{}paired\_\allowbreak{}unit\_\allowbreak{}cost}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{benchmark\_\allowbreak{}cost}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The paired per-unit cost \(C_d=c_{E,d}+c_{O,d}\) in the linear-cost CLW transport.
  \item \texttt{n\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{CLW\}\allowbreak{},\allowbreak{}B\}\allowbreak{}(d)} --- \texttt{clw\_\allowbreak{}continuous\_\allowbreak{}budget\_\allowbreak{}feasible\_\allowbreak{}sample\_\allowbreak{}size}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{benchmark\_\allowbreak{}sample\_\allowbreak{}size}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The continuous sample size \(n_{\mathrm{CLW},B}(d)=B/C_d\) under the linear paired-unit cost \(c(M_d,n)=nC_d\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}Psi\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{CLW\}\allowbreak{},\allowbreak{}d\}\allowbreak{}(P;\allowbreak{}B)} --- \texttt{clw\_\allowbreak{}continuous\_\allowbreak{}linear\_\allowbreak{}cost\_\allowbreak{}population\_\allowbreak{}criterion}; \([0,\infty)\); \texttt{named\_\allowbreak{}benchmark\_\allowbreak{}objective}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} On \(\mathfrak P_{\mathcal D}^{\mathrm{CLW}}\), the continuous named criterion \(\Psi_{\mathrm{CLW},d}(P;B)=r_d^{\mathrm{lin}}(P)/n_{\mathrm{CLW},B}(d)=C_dr_d^{\mathrm{lin}}(P)/B\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}Psi\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{CLW\}\allowbreak{},\allowbreak{}d\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{int\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}(P;\allowbreak{}B)} --- \texttt{clw\_\allowbreak{}integer\_\allowbreak{}budget\_\allowbreak{}population\_\allowbreak{}criterion}; \([0,\infty]\); \texttt{named\_\allowbreak{}benchmark\_\allowbreak{}objective}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The integer-budget criterion \(r_d^{\mathrm{lin}}(P)/\lfloor B/C_d\rfloor\) when \(\lfloor B/C_d\rfloor>0\), and \(+\infty\) otherwise.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal D\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{CLW\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}(P;\allowbreak{}B)} --- \texttt{clw\_\allowbreak{}continuous\_\allowbreak{}population\_\allowbreak{}selector}; \(2^{\mathcal D}\); \texttt{named\_\allowbreak{}benchmark\_\allowbreak{}decision}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The tie-preserving minimizer set of \(\Psi_{\mathrm{CLW},d}(P;B)\) over \(d\in\mathcal D\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathcal D\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{CLW\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{int\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}(P;\allowbreak{}B)} --- \texttt{clw\_\allowbreak{}integer\_\allowbreak{}budget\_\allowbreak{}population\_\allowbreak{}selector}; \(2^{\mathcal D}\); \texttt{named\_\allowbreak{}benchmark\_\allowbreak{}decision}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The tie-preserving minimizer set of \(\Psi_{\mathrm{CLW},d}^{\mathrm{int}}(P;B)\) over \(d\in\mathcal D\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}rho\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{CLW\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}(P;\allowbreak{}B)} --- \texttt{clw\_\allowbreak{}arm\_\allowbreak{}normalized\_\allowbreak{}design\_\allowbreak{}value\_\allowbreak{}ratio}; \([1,\infty)\); \texttt{diagnostic}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The best-tie ratio of \(\mathcal V_d^*(P)\) at the continuous CLW selector to the global minimum over \(\mathcal D\).
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}rho\_\allowbreak{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{CLW\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}\textasciicircum{}\{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}mathrm\{IKM\}\allowbreak{}\}\allowbreak{}(P;\allowbreak{}B)} --- \texttt{clw\_\allowbreak{}ikmw\_\allowbreak{}design\_\allowbreak{}value\_\allowbreak{}ratio}; \([1,\infty)\); \texttt{diagnostic}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The best-tie ratio of \(\mathcal V_{d,\mathrm{IKM}}^*(P)\) at the continuous CLW selector to its global minimum over \(\mathcal D\).
  \item \texttt{x} --- \texttt{binary\_\allowbreak{}early\_\allowbreak{}proxy\_\allowbreak{}signal}; \((0,1)\); \texttt{index}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{A generic early-\allowbreak{}proxy signal in a binary center design.}
  \item \texttt{y} --- \texttt{binary\_\allowbreak{}late\_\allowbreak{}proxy\_\allowbreak{}signal}; \((0,1)\); \texttt{index}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{A generic late-\allowbreak{}proxy signal in a binary center design.}
  \item \texttt{R(x,\allowbreak{}y)} --- \texttt{binary\_\allowbreak{}center\_\allowbreak{}residual\_\allowbreak{}variance}; \((0,1)\); \texttt{witness\_\allowbreak{}function}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The exact binary-center residual \(R(x,y)=(1-x^2)(1-y^2)/(1-x^2y^2)\), equal there to both the affine-linear CLW residual and the unrestricted unadjusted prediction risk.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}beta\_\allowbreak{}1} --- \texttt{binary\_\allowbreak{}center\_\allowbreak{}linear\_\allowbreak{}coefficient}; \(\mathbb R\); \texttt{witness\_\allowbreak{}coefficient}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The early-proxy coefficient \(\beta_1=x(1-y^2)/(1-x^2y^2)\) in the binary-center affine projection.
  \item \texttt{\textbackslash{}\allowbreak{}beta\_\allowbreak{}2} --- \texttt{binary\_\allowbreak{}center\_\allowbreak{}linear\_\allowbreak{}coefficient}; \(\mathbb R\); \texttt{witness\_\allowbreak{}coefficient}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} The late-proxy coefficient \(\beta_2=y(1-x^2)/(1-x^2y^2)\) in the binary-center affine projection.
  \item \texttt{B\_\allowbreak{}0(P)} --- \texttt{law\_\allowbreak{}specific\_\allowbreak{}floor\_\allowbreak{}budget\_\allowbreak{}threshold}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{boundary}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} \texttt{A finite law-\allowbreak{}specific threshold beyond which the strict continuous CLW ordering is preserved by the integer floor-\allowbreak{}budget selector.}
  \item \texttt{w\_\allowbreak{}d} --- \texttt{generic\_\allowbreak{}positive\_\allowbreak{}design\_\allowbreak{}profile}; \((0,\infty)\); \texttt{comparison\_\allowbreak{}profile}
  \par\smallskip\noindent\emph{Definition.} Either positive design profile \(\mathcal V_d^*(P)\) or \(\mathcal V_{d,\mathrm{IKM}}^*(P)\) in a finite-catalogue enumeration.
